=====START github backup-glue.js=====
/* Glamourless Systems backup site glue. Rewrites product links from the assessment results.
   SKU links go to the Payhip page in payhip-links.js when one is set, otherwise to checkout.html. */
(function(){
var MAP=window.GS_PAYHIP||{};
function skuOf(a){var h=a.getAttribute('href')||'';var m=h.match(/GS-\d{4}/i);if(m)return m[0].toUpperCase();
 var card=a.parentElement;for(var i=0;i<6&&card;i++){var c=card.querySelector('a[href*="checkout.html?sku="]');
 if(c){var mm=c.getAttribute('href').match(/GS-\d{4}/i);if(mm)return mm[0].toUpperCase()}card=card.parentElement}return null}
function dest(s){return MAP[s]||('checkout.html?sku='+s)}
function fix(){
 document.querySelectorAll('a[href]').forEach(function(a){if(a.getAttribute('data-gsb'))return;var h=a.getAttribute('href');
  if(/(^|\/)blueprint\/[^\/]+\.html/.test(h)||/checkout\.html\?sku=/.test(h)){var s=skuOf(a);if(s){a.setAttribute('href',dest(s));a.setAttribute('data-gsb','1')}}});
 var INTERNAL=/Internal review flags|workflow tags \(internal\)|STAGING TEST MODE|Staging checkout\.|Staging only|Flagged for human review/;
 document.querySelectorAll('body *:not(script):not(style):not(template)').forEach(function(e){if(e.children.length===0&&e.style.display!=='none'&&INTERNAL.test(e.textContent)){var b=e.closest('p,div,li,section')||e;b.style.display='none'}});
 document.querySelectorAll('.card').forEach(function(c){var b=c.querySelector('b');if(b&&/^Narrow to three/i.test(b.textContent.trim()))c.remove()});
}
var t;new MutationObserver(function(){clearTimeout(t);t=setTimeout(fix,150)}).observe(document.documentElement,{childList:true,subtree:true});
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',fix);else fix();
})();

=====END github backup-glue.js=====

of Glamourless Systems. He spent more than 15 years in consultative sales and over a decade of field work across residential construction, restoration, estimating, property inspections, and energy programs. He built his first business, a handyman and remodeling company, at 22, teaching himself web design and SEO to run it, and he has been building businesses ever since.</p><p>Glamourless Systems brings that background together: practical business blueprints and execution systems designed to help ordinary people build income they control. No hype, no guru act. Just practical systems, clearly explained, that a regular person can put to work.</p></section><div id="section-core-philosophy" data-animation-class="" data-entrance-clip="1" class="fullSection true c-section c-wrapper section-core-philosophy"><div id="gsabout"><div class="blk lead"><p class="eb">The founder</p><h2>Built by someone who has run the work</h2><p>Doug Sexton is the founder of Glamourless Systems. No hype, no guru act.</p><div class="cards"><div class="card"><h3>Understand</h3><p>Plain-language guides that show how a business actually works before you spend money on it.</p></div><div auto;"><div id="gsfaqroot"><p class="gsq-intro">Practical answers about Glamourless Systems, the Virtual Library, assessments, products, delivery, and support.</p><nav class="gsq-nav" aria-label="FAQ Business</a></nav><h2 id="faq-about">About Glamourless Systems</h2><details><summary>What is Glamourless Systems?</summary><div>Glamourless Systems publishes practical business blueprints, tools, and guided assessments for ordinary people who want to understand, start, run, or fix a real business. The goal is income you control without giving up your time. The material is educational. It is not a guaranteed income, a certification, or a done-for-you business.</div></details><details><summary>Who is Glamourless Systems for?</summary><div>It is built for people who want a plain-language path into business ownership or a fix for a business they already run: side-hustlers, first-time owners, service providers, and owners who feel stuck doing everything themselves. You do not need a business background.</div></details><details><summary>What does Glamourless Systems provide?</summary><div>Practical operating instructions, tools, and systems for understanding, starting, running, and improving a real business. These include business Blueprints, Quick Guides, supporting documents, HTML tools, implementation courses, and audio and video.</div></details><details><summary>Is Glamourless Systems a coaching or done-for-you service?</summary><div>No. The products are self-paced educational resources. Product support covers download and access problems and tool defects. It does not include business coaching, legal advice, tax advice, or implementation help.</div></details><details><summary>Does Glamourless Systems guarantee income or results?</summary><div>No. Nothing on this site promises income, profit, or a specific result. Results depend on your effort, your market, your starting point, and many things outside anyone's control. Real work is still required.</div></details><details><summary>Where is Glamourless Systems based and who does it serve?</summary><div>Glamourless Systems is based in Michigan, USA. Prices are in USD. The initial customer markets are the United States, Canada, and Mexico. Requirements that apply to you still depend on the product and your circumstances.</div></details><h2 id="faq-products">Products and Blueprints</h2><details><summary>What is a Blueprint?</summary><div>A Blueprint is a complete operating guide for one business opportunity. It explains how that kind of business works, what it takes to start, how to run it day to day, and where the common problems are. Review each product page for its exact contents.</div></details><details><summary>What is the difference between a Blueprint and a Quick Guide?</summary><div>A Blueprint covers a full operating model for a particular opportunity. A Quick Guide focuses on a narrower question or problem. Review each product's actual contents and fit boundaries. Do not assume every supporting resource is included.</div></details><details><summary>What are supporting documents?</summary><div>Supporting documents are the practical add-ons that go with a Blueprint, such as checklists, templates, scripts, and worksheets. They are sold as a set for the product they belong to.</div></details><details><summary>What is a Kit?</summary><div>A Kit bundles the parts that belong to one product in a single download: the Blueprint, supporting documents, HTML tools, implementation course, and audio and video where they exist. A Kit is priced below the sum of its parts, so buying the parts together costs less than buying them one at a time.</div></details><details><summary>What are HTML tools?</summary><div>HTML tools are small interactive calculators, planners, and trackers that open in your web browser. They run on your own device and do not need special software.</div></details><details><summary>What is the Business Accelerator Pack?</summary><div>The Business Accelerator Pack is an eight-book execution collection about customer acquisition, follow-up, sales conversations, copy, and administration. It complements a Blueprint and can also be offered on its own. It is not another opportunity assessment or a promise to fix every business problem.</div></details><details><summary>What are the free guides?</summary><div>Free guides are short downloadable resources you can get through the assessments or through links shared on social media. They do not require payment. Some are sent automatically when you finish an assessment and choose to receive your results by email.</div></details><details><summary>How do I know which product fits me?</summary><div>Take the assessment. It narrows the library to the products that match your situation. You can also search and filter the Virtual Library by business model, startup cost, and effort level.</div></details><h2 id="faq-assessments">Assessments</h2><details><summary>Which assessment should I choose?</summary><div>Start with the one public Find Your Path. Its first question asks what you are looking for and selects the appropriate questions automatically. Choose I'm not sure if your direction is unclear. You do not need to choose an internal assessment path yourself.</div></details><details><summary>What are the Start, Change, Choose, and Fix assessments?</summary><div>They are four paths. Start is for people who want to begin a business. Change is for people who want to change direction or add income. Choose is for people comparing options. Fix is for people who already run a business and want to find the one thing holding it back.</div></details><details><summary>How long does the assessment take?</summary><div>Most people finish in a few minutes. You can stop at any time.</div></details><details><summary>Do I have to give my email to take the assessment?</summary><div>No. We ask for your email only at the end, and only if you want your results sent to you.</div></details><details><summary>What happens to my answers?</summary><div>Your answers are used to match you with products. If you choose to receive your results, your email is used to send them. See the Privacy Policy for how information is handled.</div></details><details><summary>What if the recommendations do not feel right?</summary><div>Below the primary recommendations is the Deep Match option, a separate, longer, voluntarily selected assessment across the entire sellable catalog. That full matching engine is not connected yet, and this staging preview does not pretend it is complete.</div></details><details><summary>Is the assessment a guarantee that a business will work for me?</summary><div>No. It is a structured way to compare your situation to a business model. It is not financial, legal, or tax advice, and it cannot predict results.</div></details><details><summary>Can I retake an assessment?</summary><div>Yes. You can take it as many times as you like, and your recommendations may change as your answers change.</div></details><h2 id="faq-library">Virtual Library and Comparing Opportunities</h2><details><summary>What is the Virtual Library?</summary><div>The Virtual Library is a searchable catalog of business models. You can filter by startup cost, effort, skills needed, and more to compare opportunities side by side.</div></details><details><summary>How do I compare business opportunities?</summary><div>Use the Virtual Library to search and filter source-based business-model facts. Business startup costs are different from the price of a Blueprint. A comparison entry does not mean a complete package is ready to buy.</div></details><details><summary>Where do the numbers in the library come from?</summary><div>Figures are drawn from named public sources and are meant as starting points, not promises. Always verify costs and rules for your own location and circumstances.</div></details><details><summary>Is the startup cost the same as the price of a Blueprint?</summary><div>No. The startup cost is what the business itself may cost to launch. The Blueprint price is what you pay for the guide.</div></details><h2 id="faq-buying">Pricing, Delivery, and Refunds</h2><details><summary>What currency are prices in?</summary><div>All prices are in US dollars (USD).</div></details><details><summary>How are Blueprints and Kits delivered?</summary><div>Products are digital. After purchase you receive access to a download link for your files. Nothing is shipped.</div></details><details><summary>What do I get with a Kit download?</summary><div>A Kit arrives as a single zip file. Inside are folders for each part of the product that exists, such as the Blueprint, supporting documents, HTML tools, implementation course, and audio and video.</div></details><details><summary>Do I need special software to open the files?</summary><div>No. Blueprints and documents open as standard files, HTML tools open in any modern web browser, and audio and video play on common players.</div></details><details><summary>What is your refund policy?</summary><div>Please read the Refund Policy page for the current terms before you buy. It is the governing statement for refunds.</div></details><details><summary>Can I purchase through this preview?</summary><div>No. This is a staging website. Payment setup is deferred, checkout is disabled, and final packaging and release checks remain open. Preview navigation and assessment testing do not create an order.</div></details><details><summary>Are the products subscriptions?</summary><div>Products are sold as one-time digital purchases unless a product page clearly says otherwise.</div></details><h2 id="faq-support">Support, Privacy, and Terms</h2><details><summary>How do I get product support?</summary><div>Email admin@glamourlesssystems.com with the product name and, when relevant, your order reference. Product support covers download and access problems and tool defects, not business coaching or implementation advice. Do not email passwords, customer records, or unencrypted backups. No response or repair deadline is guaranteed.</div></details><details><summary>I cannot open or find my download. What should I do?</summary><div>Check your email, including spam, for your access link. If you still cannot get in, email admin@glamourlesssystems.com with the product name and your order reference.</div></details><details><summary>Where can I read the Privacy Policy and Terms?</summary><div>They are linked in the site footer. They are currently pending legal review, so treat the published text as in progress.</div></details><details><summary>Is Glamourless Systems giving legal, tax, or financial advice?</summary><div>No. All content is educational. Talk to a licensed attorney, accountant, or financial professional about decisions that depend on your situation.</div></details><details><summary>Can children use the assessments?</summary><div>The assessments are designed for adults. Handling for younger visitors is a pending legal review item.</div></details><h2 id="faq-business">Starting and Fixing a Business</h2><details><summary>How do I start a business with little money?</summary><div>Pick a business model with low startup costs, such as a service business, and start with one paying customer before spending on extras. Compare startup costs in the Virtual Library, and read the matching Blueprint to see what the first steps and costs look like. Costs vary by location and model.</div></details><details><summary>What is the best business to start?</summary><div>There is no single best business. The best fit depends on your skills, time, money, and how much risk you can accept. The assessment compares your answers to business models so you can narrow the list.</div></details><details><summary>How do I build income I control without giving up my time?</summary><div>Look for models where your time is not the product. That usually means systems, repeatable processes, and tools that do repeated work for you. It takes real effort to build, and no model removes work entirely.</div></details><details><summary>How do I fix a business that depends on me for everything?</summary><div>Find the one bottleneck first, such as follow-up, sales, delivery, or admin, then fix that before anything else. The Fix assessment is built to help you find it.</div></details><details><summary>Do I need a business plan before I start?</summary><div>A lengthy plan is not required to begin. You do need to understand your customer, your costs, and how you will get paid. A Blueprint walks through those basics for a specific business.</div></details><details><summary>How long does it take to make money from a new business?</summary><div>It varies widely and nothing here predicts a timeline. Some service businesses can land a first customer quickly. Others take months. Plan your finances around the slow case.</div></details><div class="gsq-foot">Still have a question? Email <a href="mailto:admin@glamourlesssystems.com">admin@glamourlesssystems.com</a> with the product name and, when relevant, your order reference. Educational resources only. No income or result is guaranteed.</div></div></div></div></div><footer class="gs gs-footer" data-gs-component="footer" id="gs-shared-footer"><div class="gs-wrap"><div class="gs-footer__cols"><div id="section-staging-status-section" data-animation-class="" data-entrance-clip="1" class="fullSection true c-section c-wrapper section-staging-status-section"><div id="gsrefund"><h2>Our Digital Product Promise</h2><p>At Glamourless Systems, we believe customers deserve to receive exactly what they paid for. Our products are digital and delivered electronically. Because downloadable products cannot be physically returned, purchases are generally final once delivered. We still stand behind what we sell.</p><h2>What we will fix</h2><p>Contact us if any of these happen:</p><ul><li>You received the wrong product.</li><li>A file is defective or will not open.</li><li>You cannot access your purchase or download.</li><li>Important content we advertised is missing.</li><li>You were charged twice or charged by mistake.</li></ul><h2>How to ask</h2><p>Email <a href="mailto:admin@glamourlesssystems.com">admin@glamourlesssystems.com</a> with the product name and your order reference. Please report purchase problems within 14 days of purchase. We review later reports case by case.</p><h2>What we will do</h2><p>We will work to correct the problem promptly. If we cannot provide the product as promised or fix a material defect within 7 business days of your report, we will refund the purchase to your original payment method. Duplicate and erroneous charges are corrected.</p><h2>What we do not refund</h2><p>We do not ordinarily provide refunds for a change of mind after delivery, for not using the materials, or for not achieving a particular business or financial result. Our products are educational resources, and nothing here guarantees income or any specific result.</p><h2>Free guides</h2><p>Free guides have no charge, so there is nothing to refund.</p><h2>Payment disputes</h2><p>If something is wrong with your order, please contact us first. We can usually resolve a problem faster than a bank or card dispute.</p><h2>Your rights</h2><p>Nothing in this policy limits your rights under applicable consumer protection laws.</p><div class="box"><p style="margin:0"><b>Our promise is simple:</b> you pay for a product, and we deliver that product as described, in working condition.</p></div><p class="upd">Last updated November 1, 2026.
